Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in ceramics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 264 and the invariant I(A,B) = 305 mod 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by ceramics generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 305. QED.
